## 📝 Duplicate Page Titles

Pages should have a descriptive, unique `<title>` so users can tell them apart in browser tabs, history, and bookmarks ([WCAG 2.4.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/page-titled.html)).

- **"Press | CivicActions"** — 3 pages:
  - https://civicactions.com/press/
  - https://civicactions.com/press/2/
  - https://civicactions.com/press/3/
- **"CivicActions | Open and Agile Digital Government Services | CivicActions"** — 2 pages:
  - https://civicactions.com/services2/
  - https://civicactions.com/styles/
- **"Digital IT Acquisition Professional (DITAP) certification program | CivicActions"** — 2 pages:
  - https://civicactions.com/services/ditap/
  - https://civicactions.com/services/ditap/register/

## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 45 page(s) - 90 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R73: Paragraphs of text have a line height that is not too small](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r73)
**Issue**: The line height of the paragraph is less than 1.5

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<p>...</p>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/p
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('/p')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://civicactions.com/
- https://civicactions.com/accessibility-statement/
